## Gabon added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Gabon's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **17 documents: 6 pupil textbooks (the CIAM *Mathématiques* volumes for 6e, 5e, 4e, 2e Scientifique, 1re SM and 1re Littéraire, EDICEF), 6 teacher guides (the *Super Efficace* guides pédagogiques for 1re–5e année, EDIG/EDICEF, and the CIAM *Mathématiques 2e S* guide pédagogique) and 5 curriculum documents (the IPN *Curriculum Mathématiques* for 1re–5e année)** — every file grabbed fresh for the read, its byte size and page count matching the records exactly and its identity proved from cover/title/sommaire pages — and placed 522 items: 83 pupil-textbook teaching items, 389 teacher-evidence items and 50 curriculum-requirement items. The expansion map's total of 17 files is right but its split is wrong in kind (the read's flagged claim F1): the map lists “11 student + 6 teacher”, counting the 5 IPN curriculum PDFs among the student books; the true split is 6 pupil textbooks + 6 teacher guides + 5 curriculum documents. **No *Super Efficace* pupil book is in hand at any primary grade** — the primary pupil books are print-only commercial (EDIG) and were never secured, so Gabon's primary coverage is the IPN curriculum plus the teacher guide for each year; each guide teaches every lesson of the absent pupil book and cites its pages, and the guide rows below cite both the guide page and the pupil-book page the guide gives. There are no partial volumes and no duplicates in the set: every CIAM scan runs from its first chapter to its own index at the sommaire's stated page (the 6e scan opens at printed p. 3 — the cover and title leaves are not in the scan, but all teaching content is present, printed pp. 3–222 = the PDF's 220 pages exactly). The two misleading historic Drive filenames are settled by the books' own title pages: the file named “2nde SM” is the CIAM 2e Scientifique pupil book, and the file named “2nde C” is its 2e S Guide pédagogique. Link-only CIAM volumes (3e, Terminale SM/SE and their guides) are not in hand and are cited nowhere. Counting grain: one curriculum item per palier × compétence statement; one guide item per lesson the guide teaches; one pupil item per sommaire chapter. Book errors are carried corrected where they land: the Curriculum 2e année, Compétence 2, Palier 1 prints “le passionnément des objets l'espace”, a typo for “positionnement” (positioning of objects in space), and is stocked corrected; the Curriculum 3e année, Compétence 1, Palier 2 states “nombres de 1001 à 1999 (0 à 1999)” — the parenthetical contradicts the main range, and the statement is stocked by its main range; the CIAM 4e index spells PPMC as “PPCM” (trivial, noted for the record). The placements' named exclusions — the guides' remediation/application apparatus between paliers; the curricula's front matter and framework apparatus (rationale, timetables, competency-framework and assessment sections); the CIAM pupil books' end-of-chapter exercise sets, préfaces, authors' letters and closing indexes; the 2e S pupil book's Greek-alphabet page; the 2e S guide's synoptic programme tables — are stocked nowhere. Gabon COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure on the strength of its pupil-textbook rows; its teacher-evidence and curriculum-requirement rows are named in the table but stay outside the count. ## Ireland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Ireland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **9 pupil textbooks of the Text & Tests series (The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon): one English volume, Text & Tests 4 (New Edition), and eight Irish-language (Téacs & Trialacha) volumes** — and placed 570 items. Ireland has no government-published textbooks: every book in the set is a commercial in-use title held as a complete free copy. The English TT4 was read in phase3-staging (byte- and SHA-256-verified in place); the eight Irish volumes were re-grabbed from their recorded COGG (cogg.ie) source URLs, each landing byte-exact at its recorded size, every file's identity proved from its cover, copyright and contents pages. The expansion map's count is right: **9 student + 0 teacher** — the one further Ireland file in hand, *Dúchas 2 — Leabhar Scileanna*, is a Junior Cycle Irish-language (Gaeilge) skills book, not Maths, and is cited nowhere in this course. Extent, honestly: five whole-course books (TT1, TT2 GL, TT3, English TT4, TT6); three strand volumes complete as published but covering one strand only (TT4 Irish = Strand 2; TT5 Irish = Strand 1; TT7 Irish = Strand 5); and one supplement booklet (the TT5 Forlíonadh — deferred Strand 1 material and the Central Limit Theorem), complete as published, not a standalone textbook. Two record flags ride with the set (the read's flagged claims F1–F2, both about the library records, not the course): **F1** — 8 of the 9 PDFs are absent from their phase3-staging folders, which hold only books.json + CURRICULUM.md; all eight are secured in GitHub and were re-grabbed byte-exact from COGG for the read, and restoring them into staging is owed on the library side. **F2** — the held TT3 is the updated edition (2024) of the 2013 book, with an added Chapter 19, Inferential Statistics, that no record mentions; its three items are stocked from that chapter and TT3's book entry names the edition wherever it appears. The older strand books and the English TT4 New Edition teach much of the same Leaving Certificate Higher Level ground in two languages and editions (e.g. TT6 chs. 1–2 ≈ TT4 Eng chs. 1–2; TT7 ch. 2 ≈ TT4 Eng ch. 7): each book's row is stocked as its own citation — they are distinct held books, not duplicates — while Ireland itself is counted once in this lesson's taught-in figure. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of each book. The placements' named exclusions — every book's chapter tests (“Cuir triail ort féin”), tasks, learning-experience and review sections, chapter summaries and Answers sections, and every book's prefaces, keyword lists and contents pages — are stocked nowhere. Ireland COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Ireland book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (TT1).

**Book 1:** *Téacs & Trialacha 1 (Eagrán Nua)* — TT1 (pupil textbook) — The Celtic Press / CJ Fallon; Irish edition of *Text & Tests 1: First-Year Maths* by Paul Cooke, O.D. Morris and Deborah Crean (English ©2018), ISBN 978-1-9997795-3-5; 17,831,033 bytes, 423 PDF pp. Junior Cycle, 1st year. Printed page = PDF page − 7.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Points, lines and line segments (Pointí – Línte – Mírlínte) | Ireland — *Téacs & Trialacha 1* (TT1; pupil textbook), printed p. 224 |
| Angles and parallel lines (Uillinneacha agus línte comhthreomhara) | Ireland — *Téacs & Trialacha 1* (TT1; pupil textbook), printed p. 235 |

Ireland is added to the countries line and to the acceptance count above on its pupil-textbook rows — every Ireland row in this lesson is a pupil-textbook row, so Ireland counts in full. Ireland's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED for the in-hand set**. The staging flag F1 (8 of the 9 PDFs absent from their phase3-staging folders, all secured in GitHub; restore owed) is a library-record matter — reported here, not fixed by this merge.

## Fiji added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Fiji's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 pupil textbooks of the Ministry of Education / Curriculum Development Unit series, one for each of Years 1–13** — and placed 1,012 items. The expansion map's count is exact: staging `books.json` holds exactly 13 Mathematics entries (one per year, all `kind: student`) and `phase3-push-state.json` lists all 13 as pushed; no Maths teacher guide is in hand (the AQEP Maths Guides for Lower Primary exist only as AnyFlip reader copies in the download list — not in hand, not counted). The staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, so each book was re-fetched for the read — from the repo tree (Contents API) where the pusher had stored it as a blob, else from the book's recorded `source_url` (a Wayback Machine capture of the ministry file, Beacon Media, or Google Drive) — and the grabbed copies were deleted after their placements were written. The read's nine flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) `textbooks.tsv` carries only 9 of the 13 rows (Years 1, 2, 3, 4, 7, 10, 11, 12, 13) — Years 5, 6, 8 and 9 are staged and pushed but missing from the TSV; (2) the Year 2 scan duplicates printed p. 57 and printed p. 58 is absent, so the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand to read and is cited nowhere; (3) the Year 5 capture has no cover/title leaves — the file opens at the Strand 1 divider and the body runs complete, Strand 1 p. 1 to Strand 5 p. 98, its identity resting on the staged record and the body's own structure; (4) Year 4's folio sequence is irregular — folios 44–48 and 54 never appear and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice — but the leaf count closes exactly (85 printed numbers − 6 absent + 2 duplicated = 81 body leaves), every contents strand and unit is present and no activity breaks mid-stream at the gaps, so the evidence points to the printed book's own folio misprints rather than missing leaves, a judgement the file alone cannot prove; (5) Year 3's scan order is scrambled at the end (contents folio ii and printed pp. 46, 51, 52 sit at PDF pp. 76–80) and one activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51 — counted once), with folio tracking accounting for all of printed pp. 1–75; (6) Year 10 exists in two packagings — the staged Beacon Media file (9,099,595 bytes, 202 pp.) is the copy cited; an ECopies capture of the same book is 9,079,205 bytes / 200 pp.; (7) Year 12's first Wayback transfer arrived truncated (11,794,148 bytes, broken cross-reference table) and was discarded — a fresh download delivered the complete book (17,085,310 bytes, 284 pp.), which is what was read; (8) Year 1's contents lists Chance at p. 65 and Data at p. 66 where the folios are 64 and 65 — the body count closes exactly, so nothing is missing and the contents is one out; (9) Year 6's interior copyright and acknowledgement pages misname it the “Year 7” book — a copy-paste error in the book itself; its cover metadata, contents, running heads and body are Year 6 throughout, and it is read and placed as Year 6. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach — among them Year 3's conversion-table lines “1,000 cm = 1 km” (correct: 100,000 cm) and “10,000 mm = 1,000 m” (correct: 10 m), Year 4's banner “30 minutes = 1 hour” against its own body's 60, Year 5's “1 000 millimetres = 1 litre”, Year 9's contents “Common Multitudes” (for Common Multiples) and its (a × b)ᵐ = aᵐ × bⁿ misprint, and Year 1's count-in-twos strip printing 9 where 19 belongs; Year 10 carries its own printed errata list (pp. 201–202), whose corrections are noted in the items. Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching point — a named concept, method, rule or worked skill with its printed page. The placements' named exclusions — strand divider pages; contents and acknowledgement pages; pure exercise sets and worked-example repeats of an item already placed; Year 1's “Checking my number” ten-frame reference page (printed p. 32); Year 3's duplicated capacity page (counted once); Year 4's closing multiplication-table chart (printed p. 86); Year 10's printed errata list (pp. 201–202, apparatus — its corrections ride in the items); Year 12's closing motivational quotes and exam tips (printed pp. 273–275); Year 13's bibliography and back matter — are stocked nowhere. Fiji COUNTS in this lesson's taught-in figure: every Fiji book is a pupil textbook. Into this lesson the placements stock 9 items, every one a pupil-textbook row, from Book 1 (*Mathematics Year 1*), Book 2 (*Mathematics Year 2*), Book 3 (*Mathematics Year 3*), Book 4 (*Mathematics Year 4*), Book 5 (*Year 5 Mathematics*), Book 6 (*Year 6 Mathematics Textbook*), Book 7 (*Form 3 Mathematics Textbook*).

**Book 2:** *Mathematics Year 2* — the book calls itself the Year 2 Mathematics Activity book — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 233,595,104 bytes, 70 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Scan defect: printed p. 57 appears twice (PDF pp. 59–60 are the same leaf) and printed p. 58 is absent — the Money unit's Activity 12 is not in hand and is cited nowhere.

**Book 3:** *Mathematics Year 3* — Ministry of Education, Curriculum Development Unit, Revised 2015 (© 2013); 37,315,079 bytes, 80 PDF pp.; image-only scan read from rendered pages. Printed page = PDF page − 3 for the in-sequence body; the scan order is scrambled at the end and the capacity-discovery activity page appears twice (folioed 47 and 51), cited once.

Folios cited as printed in the book; the folio sequence is irregular (folios 44–48 and 54 never appear, and folios 42 and 55 each appear twice) — the leaf count closes exactly and every contents strand and unit is present, so the gaps are the book's own folio misprints, not missing leaves.

## Honduras added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Honduras's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **21 files = 18 distinct works: 9 pupil textbooks (Grades 3–9, Grade 10 Matemática I and Grade 11 Matemática III BCH) and 12 teacher-guide files for Grades 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 9, 10 and 11, the Grade 8 guide held in two editions (2018 and the 2022 revision) and the Grade 1 and 2 guides each in two scans** — all from the Secretaría de Educación's PROMETAM / UPNFM series produced with JICA support — and placed 335 items (218 pupil-textbook items and 117 teacher-evidence items), plus 3 duplicate-edition records (placements items 241, 242 and 338) that are cited once with their works and carry no separate items. The expansion map's “9 student + 12 teacher” count is right: the staging `books.json` files, the `phase3-push-state.json` pushed set and `textbooks.tsv` agree on exactly these 21 files. There is no Grade 1–2 pupil textbook in the Secretaría's programme (workbook only — SE Manual de Lineamientos), so the Grade 1–2 teaching in hand is the two guides; there is no Grade 3 or Grade 6 teacher guide in hand. Every file was grabbed from its recorded `source_url` (educatrachos.hn / libroshonduras.com) after the staged PDFs had been pushed and deleted locally, and verified by exact byte size against the download-list record and by page count against the `books.json` record before reading; grabbed copies were deleted after each book's text was extracted. Read basis, stated honestly: the six text-layer pupil books and eight text-layer guides were read in full from their text; the three scanned pupil books (Grades 7–9) were read by OCR of their Índices and unit/lesson openers with interior pages OCR-sampled — Grades 7 and 8 cross-checked against the full text-layer read of their own teacher guides, which plan every lesson class by class — and the two 2022 guides are themselves scans, OCR-read at cover, presentation and index level, the Grade 9 guide therefore placed at unit grain. Counting grain: one item per Índice lesson; a lesson teaching two distinct course topics is split into two items, and three items (a Grade 4 pupil lesson, a Grade 10 pupil lesson and a Grade 2 guide unit) each carry a row in two lessons below and are counted once. The placements' named exclusions — the “Páginas para recortar” cut-out pages of the Grades 3, 4 and 5 pupil books (manipulatives, not teaching text), the Grade 6 book's closing “Repaso” review, and the “Nos divertimos” / “¿Sabías que?” enrichment boxes inside placed lessons — are stocked nowhere. The read's flagged claims ride with the set, honestly: (1) the download list §A's original 2026-10-21 mirror labels for the III-Ciclo files were swapped between Español and Matemáticas — the files it calls “Matemáticas 7/8/9 (2018 PROMETAM)” are the Español books, and its “Español 7/8/9” alternates are the Matemáticas books — a library-record fault, already corrected in `books.json`, reported here and not fixed by this merge; (2) the Grade 9 guide's cover misprints its cycle as “II Ciclo” where the programme is III Ciclo; (3) the Grade 11 pupil book's Índice misprints “Cincunferencias” for “Circunferencias”, taught corrected; (4) the 2014 scan of the Grade 1 guide has a materially poorer text layer than the mirror scan of the same work, so anyone text-mining it alone will under-read it. Book errors found in the read are taught corrected, the corrected form being what the items teach. Into this lesson the placements stock 12 items — 6 pupil-textbook rows and 6 teacher-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 3er grado*), Book 7 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 1er grado*), Book 8 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 2do grado*), Book 13 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 7mo grado (2018)*), Book 14 (*Guía del Docente, Matemáticas, 8vo grado (2018)*), Book 15 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas, 7mo grado*), Book 16 (*Libro del Estudiante, Matemáticas and 8vo grado*).

Book errors and faults are carried as the placements record them, taught corrected where they touch a row: the Grade 1 fractions standard (printed p. 275) cross-refers an objective as “Objective 2.3.27” — a Grade 2 bank number inside the Grade 1 book — and objective numbers with a leading “2.” recur throughout the Grade 1 standards (the bank numbering is shared across grades; ## Serbia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Serbia's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — **one pupil textbook**: *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school), Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018 — and placed **95 teaching items** from it. The book is a genuine, complete pupil textbook (уџбеник), not a workbook, not an exam paper and not an excerpt: every topic is presented, named and worked before it is practised. It is the only Serbia Maths book in hand — the expansion map's count and kind split (1 student, 0 teacher) are exact, and every record agrees. The file was read visually from full-page renders, every page, because its text layer is empty (a print-image PDF). It is the COMPLETE book: the CIP record gives 103 pages, the contents lists all seven units ending at printed p. 103, and the text runs printed pp. 1–103 followed by the imprint page (PDF p. 104). Printed-page basis: **printed page = PDF page** for the whole body, verified against printed folios throughout. Counting grain: one item per distinct taught skill or concept, at the point the book teaches it. The book's revision spreads (ОВО ЗНАМ / НАУЧИЛИ СМО) and its practice set (ЗАДАЦИ ЗА ВЕЖБАЊЕ) restate taught items and are named exclusions — except three places where a revision spread is the book's first presentation of new material (the number line and capacity-by-pouring on printed pp. 48–49, and balance-equivalence on printed p. 82), which are counted as teaching items and marked as such; in-lesson exercise sets are apparatus, not items. Also stocked nowhere: the front cover, contents, the seven unit divider pages and the imprint page, and the road-safety asides on printed p. 11 (no maths content). No book error was found in the book: its worked examples check out, and the contents page numbers agree with the printed folios throughout — a sheet-scale reading of printed p. 73 that seemed to omit 12 from the list of the numbers of the second ten was disproved on full-resolution re-check (the list is complete, 11, 12, 13, …, 20), an artefact of the reading scale, not a book error. Boundary, cited nowhere: Serbia's other 16 Maths files in the Kavčić free library (Grades 1–8 workbooks, textbooks and part-volumes, including the Grade 1 workbook *Математика 1 — радна свеска*) are link-only, not in hand and not read. One record-side note (reported, not fixed — merges never edit the records, and no maths/books/serbia.md filing exists or was created): textbooks.tsv secures only 6 of the 7 pushed Serbia books — the Grade 4 Serbian grammar book is in books.json and in the push state but has no TSV row; it is not a maths book and does not affect this reckoning. Into this lesson the placements stock 6 rows — all from Book 1.

**Book 1:** *Математика 1* (Matematika 1 — Mathematics for the first grade of primary school) (pupil textbook) — Serbia, Нова школа (Nova škola), Belgrade, first edition 2018; authors Милица Ћук, Бранислав Милошевић, Бранко Марковић; ISBN 978-86-6225-041-4; ministry approval решение 650-02-00208/2018-07 of 27. 4. 2018; 135,143,758 bytes, 104 PDF pp., MD5 9a5df48784a319517cff29b5b407d0b2. Complete book: printed pp. 1–103 + covers and imprint; printed page = PDF page throughout. Read visually from full-page renders (the file's text layer is empty). PUPIL TEXTBOOK — Serbia is counted in this lesson's taught-in figure.

## Haiti added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Haiti's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **28 files, every one of them teacher-side**: the nine *Programme à Compétences Minimales* (PCM) volumes for Années Fondamentales 1–9 (MENFP, Direction de l'Enseignement Fondamental, November 2019), the three IPN *Programme détaillé, Matières de base* volumes (4e, 5e, 6e année), the three *Programme Pédagogique Opérationnel* (PPO) Mathématiques fascicules for the 3e cycle (7e, 8e, 9e année, 1989-1990), the eight Nectar (MENFP) teacher sequences (four for 7e année, four for 8e année), the Nouveau Secondaire *Programme-Cadre* and the four NS detailed programmes (NS1–NS4) — every one of the 28 carrying a full Mathématiques section — and placed **1,639 items, every one of them TEACHER/CURRICULUM EVIDENCE**: **Haiti has no pupil Maths textbook in hand under any label**, so Haiti is counted in NO lesson's taught-in figure and is named in the countries line and in the rows below only, on the standing evidence-only precedent (Ghana / Bahamas / Canada / Myanmar). The expansion map's count for Haiti is wrong: it carries 15, counting only the books.json entries whose subject field reads “Mathematics”, and the download list claims 43 teacher files; the true maths-bearing set is 28 files — the nine “All subjects” PCM volumes, the three IPN detailed programmes and the NS Programme-Cadre all carry full maths sections the count missed (a record-side fault, reported, not fixed by this merge); textbooks.tsv is likewise a stale 28-row index naming only a subset of the secured files. Haiti's official pupil books (the MENFP/FAGEM *Manuels uniques* / “Mon manuel” series) are in hand for other subjects only, never for Mathematics, and the two remanié programme leads (a 7e année fascicule and a 4e année detailed programme) are not in hand — and programme documents are not textbooks under the 2026-11-10 textbook-test ruling in any case — so **Haiti stays PARTIAL for Maths, teacher evidence only**. All 28 files were read page-by-page from the secured PDFs in phase3-staging, byte-exact against their books.json records; nothing was downloaded. Read depth: the PPO 8e fascicule and the IPN 4e and 6e programmes are image-only scans and were read by OCR in full, spot-checked against the page images — where OCR garbled a worked example's digits, the placement teaches the corrected arithmetic and the case is flagged; PPO 7e PDF p. 48 and PPO 9e PDF pp. 20, 30, 31 and 32 were verified blank, separator or title pages carrying no objectives. Printed-page bases: PCM printed page = PDF page − 1; IPN 4e and 6e printed page = PDF page − 9, IPN 5e printed folios equal PDF pages; PPO 7e printed = PDF − 3, PPO 8e printed folio = PDF page, PPO 9e printed = PDF − 2; Programme-Cadre NS printed = PDF − 9 (verified against the detailed programme at §5.1–§5.2); NS1 and NS2 printed = PDF page, NS3 cites PDF pages, NS4 printed = PDF − 1; the Nectar sequences cite PDF pages by séance. The placements' eleven flags are all document-side and are carried here exactly: the PCM 6e volume's maths section header mislabels its level “5ème AF” (F1 — its items are cited to the 6e volume they stand in); PCM 5e's spoken-number row slips (“six mille deux” for 6,002, and a thousands-family slip in item 352's row) are placed corrected (F2); the PCM “orthocentre” definition error stands in the PCM **7e** volume — the diff's “(AF8)” label is the diff's own slip, disproven against the live PCM 8e section, which carries the correct circumcentre definition (F3); PCM 9e's right-asymptote and strict-inequality rows are completions of points the volume leaves silent, not errors (F4); PCM 9e's opening “années diagnostiques” ((6e), 7e, 8e) reinstate the diagnostic year the 3e-cycle fascicules dropped (F5); the PCM 7e volume announces a simple-interest objective that does not exist anywhere in its grids — nothing is placed for it, and simple interest is taught by PCM 9e and PPO 9e (F6); the Nectar “Relations entre les angles” sequence states a false corresponding-angles criterion and a mise en train with no solution, and the “Calcul littéral” sequence's séance 5 example drops a factor of x — all placed as corrected (F7, F8); the record-side faults (the map's count, the download list's count, the stale textbooks.tsv) are reported, not fixed (F9); the IPN 6e worked examples carry numerical inconsistencies (objectives 5.1, 7.1, 12.2, 16.1, 19.1, 21.1, 22.1) flagged as read and NOT asserted as print errors — the volume is an OCR-read scan and some may be digit misreads (F10); and NS2's own dating (2015 in the detailed programmes' shared headers, the school year 2008-2009 on its cover) is the book's own, not a discrepancy (F11). The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the PCM volumes' other-discipline sections and their week-by-week scheduling columns beyond each distinct objective; the IPN volumes' maths avant-propos, theme indexes, other-subject sections and Suggestions/Évaluation apparatus (except where an activity is itself the taught content); the PPO fascicules' shared front matter, Programme-Cadre theme tables, progression grids, bibliographies and annexes; the Nectar sequences' summative-evaluation séances and session-management apparatus (except where a séance's activity is the taught content); the Programme-Cadre's other subjects, and the Nouveau Secondaire framework chapters shared between it and the NS1/NS2 volumes (placed once, from the Programme-Cadre volume); and the verified blank and separator pages. Into this lesson the placements stock 63 items — all teacher/curriculum-evidence rows — from Book 1 (*PCM, 1ère AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 2 (*PCM, 2ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 3 (*PCM, 3ème AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 4 (*PCM, 4e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 5 (*PCM, 5e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 6 (*PCM, 6e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 7 (*PCM, 7e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 8 (*PCM, 8e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 9 (*PCM, 9e AF (MENFP, 2019)*), Book 10 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 4e année*), Book 11 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 5e année*), Book 12 (*IPN Programme détaillé, 6e année*), Book 13 (*PPO Mathématiques, 7e année (1989-1990)*), Book 14 (*PPO Mathématiques, 8e année (1989-1990)*) and Book 15 (*PPO Mathématiques, 9e année (1989-1990)*).

## Papua New Guinea added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Papua New Guinea’s whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **32 files = 28 works, all Papua New Guinea Department of Education (NDoE), “Issued free to schools by the Department of Education”: 8 pupil textbook files (7 works — the JICA/QUIS-ME National Mathematics Textbooks, Grades 1–6, with Grades 3 and 5 in two parts each) and 24 teacher files (21 works — 9 JICA teacher’s manual files, 6 works with Grades 3–5 in two parts each, and 15 Standards Based Curriculum (SBC) teacher guides, Elementary Prep to Grade 12, with separate Advanced and General guides at Grades 11 and 12)** — and placed 503 items (102 from the pupil textbooks, 401 teacher-evidence items from the manuals and guides). The expansion map is wrong on both of its numbers (flag 1 — record-side, reported, not fixed by this merge): it said 6 student + 22 teacher in hand; the true set is 8 student + 24 teacher. The map missed three files that exist only as textbooks.tsv rows (the Grade 4 and Grade 6 full-year JICA pupil textbooks and the Grade 6 JICA manual) and did not know the Grade 2 SBC guide, which has a staging books.json row but no textbooks.tsv row and no push-state entry (flag 4). **Filing error (flag 2 — record-side): the git-tree copies recorded for the Grade 1 and Grade 2 JICA manuals are a different country’s book** — both TSV tree paths hold the identical file, a Mozambique Grade 1 teacher manual in Portuguese (“Manual do Professor, 1.ª classe”, 5,891,019 B / 216 pp, the same MD5 at both paths — the only byte-identical duplicate pair found in this hunt, and not a PNG book), which also sits in the release `mid-size-books-8` correctly named as a Mozambique book. The genuine PNG manuals were read from the GitHub release `full-quality-oversized-books`, whose assets match the staged sizes and checklist MD5s exactly; the push-state’s per-folder landing claims for those two files should be treated as unreliable until the tree paths are re-filed. Flag 3: the two manuals’ recorded education.gov.pg source URLs do not serve the staged pressings — both returned an HTML page during the read, and a later re-probe served a different pressing of the Grade 1 manual — so the staged pressings cannot be re-grabbed from the recorded source URLs. Flag 5: the Grade 9 SBC guide’s textbooks.tsv tree path 404s; the file was recovered by searching all release assets and matches books.json on size and pages. No Maths hides under other subject labels in the PNG records — all 156 staging books.json entries and all 163 textbooks.tsv rows were checked by subject, title and path. Link-only Maths not in hand and not read (it does not count against closure): the FODE distance course-books for Grades 7–12 Mathematics / Maths A / Maths G (unit PDFs) and the two OBE upper-secondary guides found 2026-11-10 (Advanced Mathematics TG, ISBN 978-9980-9924-8-2; General Mathematics TG). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 1 Textbook and its manual print “Subtracation” in the Chapter/Unit 12 title; the SBC guides print “Angels” for Angles in places, “Algebrain” for Algebraic (a Grade 11 guide’s Factorisation unit header) and “Orobability” for Probability (the Grade 9 guide’s strand table); the Grade 1 SBC guide’s Term-1 record prints “tenths” where tens is meant. The Grade 3 SBC programme has no Money topic where the JICA Grade 3 textbook carries one — a programme difference, not an error. Printed-page basis: JICA textbook pages cite the book’s own printed folio / contents page; JICA manuals cite the manual’s own printed contents page; SBC guides cite the guide’s printed programme/contents pages (the basis is stated in each book entry below); where a contents extraction was partly two-column-interleaved, the PDF page is given as well and marked “PDF p.”. Counting grain: one item per chapter (pupil textbooks), per unit (teacher’s manuals — the manual unit mirrors the textbook chapter of the same number and is counted once, as teacher evidence), and per named topic/unit in the SBC guides’ own programmes. The placements’ named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the end-of-book/year “Summary” chapters and units in the JICA textbooks and manuals (review apparatus), and the SBC guides’ front sections, assessment-record templates, glossaries/resources and integrated cross-subject project sections. Pupil-textbook rows are COUNTED normally; every manual and SBC guide row is teacher evidence — named in the countries line and in the rows, but outside the acceptance count. Into this lesson the placements stock 1 item — 0 pupil-textbook rows and 1 teacher-evidence row — from Book T14 (*Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 4 (SBC)*).

**Book T14:** *Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 4* (SBC teacher guide, Junior Primary) — Papua New Guinea, Department of Education (NDoE), Standards Based Curriculum; 33,896,565 bytes, 158 PDF pp. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. Printed-page basis: the guide’s Yearly Overview (printed pp. 9–12); sample guided lessons, as with Grade 3.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| Perpendicular and parallel lines | Papua New Guinea — *Mathematics Teacher Guide Grade 4 (SBC)* (SBC teacher guide — teacher evidence), the guide’s Yearly Overview, printed pp. 9–12 |

Papua New Guinea is added to the countries line only — this lesson holds Papua New Guinea teacher-evidence rows only (JICA teacher’s manuals and SBC teacher guides), so the acceptance count above is unchanged; the rows are teacher evidence, not pupil-taught content. Papua New Guinea’s class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every in-hand Papua New Guinea Maths book (all 32 files) has been read in full and stocked.

## Switzerland added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Switzerland's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **8 files / 6 works, and no pupil textbook among them**: the three PER brochures *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales*, Cycles 1, 2 and 3 (CIIP, Version 3.0, août 2024), whose Mathématiques parts are placed here; the Ticino *Piano di studio della scuola dell'obbligo ticinese* (DECS, edizione perfezionata 2022), whose Matematica chapter is placed here; the *Lehrplan 21 Graubünden* (Fassung vom 15.03.2016), whose Mathematik plan is placed here — one work in three filed language editions (German, Romansh, Italian), read in full in the German edition, verified identical in structure and pagination in the other two, and cited once; and one teacher guide, *MaMa — Linee guida del progetto MaMa: Ambito Numeri e calcolo* (DECS/CeRDD, 2021), whose didactic rulings are placed here as teacher evidence — 425 items in all (364 curriculum evidence + 61 teacher-guide evidence, 0 pupil). The expansion map's kind split is WRONG (record-level; the records themselves are not edited — merges never edit the records): it recorded "3 student + 1 teacher", but the three "student" files are the PER **curriculum** brochures mislabelled — **Switzerland has 0 pupil Maths textbooks in hand**, and its prescribed pupil series (Schweizer Zahlenbuch, Mathematik Primarstufe / Sekundarstufe I (LMVZ), Mathwelt, Mathbuch, MER Mathématiques 1–11, Ticino's Base Matematica 1–4) are commercial and licence-gated, not in hand and not read. Further maths was found hiding under "all subjects" labels: the Ticino Piano and the Lehrplan 21 Graubünden are all-subjects volumes carrying full mathematics chapters. Every grabbed file was verified SHA-256 byte-exact against its books.json record. Checked in hand and excluded with reasons, stocked nowhere: the three PER *Aperçus des contenus* (one example-grain maths page each — record items R1–R3), the PER *Présentation générale* (framework structure only), the PER *Éducation numérique* brochure (the computer-science curriculum; mathematics appears only as cross-links — a separate school subject) and the four Vaud *<DÉ>CODAGE* digital-education teacher manuals (contents pages of all four read: Science informatique / Médias / Usages only — a separate school subject); inside the PER brochures, the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts are other subjects, the *Modélisation* objectives are process apparatus whose content is carried by the per-theme problem-solving items, and the Lexiques are apparatus; inside the Ticino volume, every chapter other than Matematica is another subject and the process/attitude competence targets carry no subject content; inside the Lehrplan 21 volumes, every subject other than Mathematik is out of scope and the Einführung/Gesamtüberblick chapters are structure, not content; MaMa §§1 and 3 are project apparatus, and §2's quoted Ticino targets are placed at the Ticino items, not double-counted (record item R4). One within-document inconsistency is flagged so it is not stocked: the PER Cycle 1 *Aperçus des contenus* claims Cycle 1 pupils "calculate lengths (perimeter of a rectangle), areas, volumes (cube)" — the governing Cycle 1 brochure requires only comparison and measurement with an étalon (items 46–55), and perimeter/area/volume calculation begins in Cycle 2 (items 158–160); the brochure governs, the Aperçu's Cycle 1 sentence is an overstatement, and it is NOT stocked as a Cycle 1 requirement (record item R1). One document error is called out by a source itself and taught corrected: the MaMa guide rules that there is no "dissociative property" of addition — splitting an addend and associating (16 + 19 = 16 + 10 + 9) is the associative property, and textbooks that name it otherwise are wrong (item 378); the guide's other flagged classroom errors are misconceptions it warns against, not errors it commits. No Liechtenstein file is judged here: Liechtenstein teaches from Switzerland's books, but no file is filed under Liechtenstein in any record. Printed-page basis: PER brochures, Ticino Piano and Lehrplan 21 — printed folio = PDF page − 2; MaMa — printed page = PDF page. Switzerland's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: every in-hand Maths-bearing file has been read and stocked, but all 425 of its items are curriculum or teacher-guide evidence and no pupil textbook is in hand. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 items (2 curriculum evidence) from Book 2. Switzerland is added to the countries line and named in the rows below as evidence only, and is NOT added to the acceptance count, on the standing evidence-only precedent.

**Book 2:** *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales — Cycle 2* (curriculum brochure) — Switzerland, CIIP (Conférence intercantonale de l'instruction publique de la Suisse romande et du Tessin), Plan d'études romand (PER), Version 3.0, août 2024; 1,209,759 bytes, 116 PDF pp.; SHA-256 verified exact against its books.json record. Printed folio = PDF p. − 2. Only the Mathématiques part (printed pp. 14–29) is placed here; the Sciences de la nature and Sciences humaines et sociales parts of the same brochure are other subjects. Filed in the records as a student book — wrong: it is a curriculum brochure. CURRICULUM EVIDENCE ONLY — the brochure states what must be taught and teaches nothing itself; its rows are worded as the requirements they are and are counted in no lesson's taught-in figure.

| Lesson part | Where it comes from |
|---|---|
| PER Cycle 2 requires recognising parallelism and perpendicularity | Switzerland — *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales — Cycle 2* (PER curriculum brochure) — curriculum evidence only, MSN 21 (Espace), printed pp. 14–17 |
| PER Cycle 2 requires, in 7e–8e, constructing parallel and perpendicular lines | Switzerland — *Mathématiques et Sciences de la nature / Sciences humaines et sociales — Cycle 2* (PER curriculum brochure) — curriculum evidence only, MSN 21 (Espace), printed pp. 14–17 |

Switzerland enters this lesson as evidence only: it is named in the countries line and in the rows above, but it is NOT added to the acceptance count — Switzerland has no pupil Maths textbook in hand; its in-hand set is three curriculum brochures, the mathematics chapters of two all-subjects curriculum volumes and one teacher guide, which state requirements and teaching guidance without being pupil textbooks (the standing evidence-only precedent). Switzerland's class for Maths is PARTIAL (evidence-only): the whole in-hand set is read and stocked, and it advances only when a Swiss pupil Maths textbook — one of the prescribed commercial series, today licence-gated — lands in hand and is read.

## Timor-Leste added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Timor-Leste's whole in-hand library from the PDFs — **5 files**, of which **2 carry mathematics**: the Grade 5 pupil exercise book *Livru Ezersísiu Matemátika 5* and the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) of *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2*, the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume. The other three files were checked in full and carry no maths teaching: *Livru Dalan Tetun ba Klase 5* (a Tetum grammar book — number words appear once, as one of eight grammatical word-classes), *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 6 Períodu 2* (Natural and Social Science plans only; its four “matemátika” mentions sit inside one density lesson) and *Um Mundo de Histórias 6º ano* (a Portuguese reader; maths appears only inside stories). The expansion map's “1 student + 0 teacher” split counts only the books filed as Maths and repeats a filing error — **flag F1** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/timor-leste.md` filing exists): the Grade 3 volume is filed as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide”, claiming Natural Science plans from p. 10 and Social Science plans from p. 468, but its own Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components and puts Matemátika at p. 318, Siénsia Naturál at p. 512 and Siénsia Sosiál at p. 624 — the mis-filing hid the country's only in-hand Maths teacher evidence. **Flag F2** (record gap): `textbooks.tsv` carries only 3 Timor-Leste rows — the two Grade 5 books, including the Maths exercise book, sit in `books.json`, the push state and the GitHub repo tree with no TSV row. Everything else Timor-Leste Maths is link-only and not in hand — the Matemática G1–G4 student books, the G10–G12 student manuals, the 2024 new-curriculum Grade 7 set and the Learning Passport book courses — and is cited nowhere. The placements number **177 items: 83 pupil-workbook items and 94 teacher-evidence items** (33 from full printed lesson plans; 61 from the period plan table alone, each marked “(plan table only)”). The workbook is a genuine pupil teaching book — 77 numbered exercise sets (Ezersísiu 1–77) plus two front-matter reading tables — but it practises and tests; it does not present topics first (its letter to teachers states the exercises follow the annual plan and lesson-plan sequence), and it is not the main Grade 5 textbook. The guide's Matemátika section is **Period 2 only** — the Period 1 and Period 3 volumes are not in hand, so Grade 3 coverage here is one period of three. The books' own errors are taught corrected: **E1** — the workbook's census table heads its second column “2005”, but its figures are the 2010 census and its questions ask about 2010; **E2** — Ezersísiu 70 is a reprint of Ezersísiu 68, counted once (as item 70); **E3** — Ezersísiu 60's fourth prism has equal dimensions in the Tetum text and different dimensions in the Portuguese (the Tetum governs); **E4** — the guide's Lisaun 51 plan tells the teacher to write “6 x 1 = 1” (read 6 x 1 = 6, as the page's own explanation gives); **E5** — the guide's introduction states 2 formal formative assessments per period while the plan schedules 6 (assessments are exclusions either way); **E6** — ten blank plan-table content cells are continuation/test rows, folded into their parent items, and lesson 84's body settles it as the fraction–decimal relation lesson. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the workbook's covers, imprint, forewords, letters and “do not write in this book” notice; the guide's discipline introduction and assessment guidance (printed pp. 326–328); its 22 consolidation lessons, 6 formal-assessment lessons and 5 project lessons (no new content stated for any of them); blank continuation/test rows; homework and in-lesson revision steps; and the Grade 3 volume's other eight subject sections. Counting grain: pupil book — one item per distinct practised skill; teacher guide — one item per distinct taught content in the period plan. Timor-Leste's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — a pupil-workbook set on the Peru pattern: the workbook's rows count, the guide's rows are teacher evidence and count nowhere. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 items: 0 pupil-workbook + 2 teacher evidence. Every Timor-Leste row in this lesson is teacher evidence, so Timor-Leste does not count here.

**Book 1:** *Livru Planu Lisaun Klase 3 Períodu 2* (teacher guide — the combined all-subject Grade 3 Period-2 lesson-plan volume; its Matemátika section only) — Ministério da Educação de Timor-Leste; 15,881,272 bytes, 829 PDF pp., MD5 18b0a4e0d55af5f56868c556dc9893f2. Its Índise (printed p. 3) lists all nine basic-education components; the Matemátika section (printed pp. 318–511) was read in full — full 50-minute lesson plans (objective, materials, timed teaching steps; the MAT3.x codes are the national curriculum's Grade 3 outcomes) plus the period plan table at printed pp. 318–325. Printed page = PDF page. TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY — its rows are named in a lesson but counted in no taught-in figure, and the section is Period 2 only. Filed in the library records as the “Ciências Naturais Grade 3 Teacher Guide” — the filing error F1 carried in the intro above.

## Uganda added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Uganda's whole in-hand Maths library from the PDFs — **8 files / 8 works: 7 pupil books + 1 teacher's guide**, all Uganda Ministry of Education and Sports / NCDC *Home-Study Learning* books (August 2020) — and the expansion map's “7 student + 1 teacher, FILES-IN-HAND” count and split are **correct**, with one correction of kind: the Primary Five pupil book is not a standalone Maths book — it is the Mathematics booklet (package PDF pp. 9–166) inside the NCDC Primary Five merged home-study package (Mathematics + Integrated Science + English + Social Studies, 350 pp.). Maths content hiding under an all-subjects label was checked for across the whole in-hand set; this package is the only such volume. The seven pupil books (Primary Four, the Primary Five booklet, Primary Six, Senior One, Senior Two, Senior Three, Senior Four) are real pupil teaching books — lessons, worked examples and exercises addressed to the learner — so Uganda's pupil rows **count** in a lesson's taught-in figure. The one teacher book, the Senior One *Teacher's Guide* (prototype, 76 pp.), is **teacher evidence only** and counts nowhere; it covers all 12 Senior One prototype topics, including five topics (1 Number Bases, 2 Working with Integers, 8 Angle Properties, 9 Data Collection and Presentation, 12 Time and Time Tables) for which no Senior One pupil text is in hand, and its evidence for those topics carries that “no pupil book in hand” caveat. NOT in hand, and cited nowhere: the Senior One prototype pupil Mathematics textbook (NCDC posted only the Teacher's Guide) and the Scribd combined S1&S2 Science Package (no download route). The placements number **324 items: 278 pupil items and 46 teacher-evidence items (TG1–TG46)** — one item per distinct lesson in the home-study books, one per distinct topic treatment in the guide. Page citations are PDF pages for the pupil books (in the primary home-study books the printed folios run 6 behind: printed = PDF − 6; Primary Five pages are the merged package's PDF pages) and the guide's printed pages for the TG items. The books' own errors are taught corrected: the Senior One home-study book heads the conversion example at PDF p. 8 “Convert 13/4 into an improper fraction” — 13/4 is already improper, and the worked example in fact converts it to the mixed number 3¼; the Primary Six book's triangle-angles exercise (PDF p. 85) prints two interior angles as “360 and 540” — the degree signs set as zeros, read as 36° and 54°; the Senior Three book heads its mensuration topic “TRIGONOMETRY” while its own stated outcomes and lessons are units, surface area and volume — taught as mensuration. Uganda's own ways are kept: equations written with a shape for the unknown before letters (Primary Four); integers walked on a ground number line, face forward for positive and back for backward (Primary Five and Six); base five taught in primary school (Primary Five); fractions taught by slicing chapattis (Senior One); income tax worked from Uganda's own tax bands and allowances (Senior Three). Uganda's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** — every Maths book in hand has been read fresh and placed; what remains outside the set (the Senior One prototype pupil textbook, the Senior Two–Four prototype books and guides) is not in hand and is a library hunting matter, not a course hold. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 pupil items.

**Book 1:** *Home-Study Learning, Primary Five* — the Mathematics booklet only (pupil book) — Uganda, Ministry of Education and Sports / NCDC, August 2020; the Primary Five book is not a standalone Maths book: it is the Mathematics booklet (package PDF pp. 9–166, 158 pp.) inside the merged home-study package (Mathematics + Integrated Science + English + Social Studies); the package file is 10,444,968 bytes, 350 PDF pp., MD5 5159920c66cfd1869ff19fd9cf119fea, and the booklet's interior is verified as the Primary Five Mathematics home-study book. Pages are cited by the package's PDF page. PUPIL BOOK — its rows count in a lesson's taught-in figure.

Named exclusions, stocked nowhere: the answers sections of every pupil book; Year 13's “Problems and Investigations” end-of-chapter sections, its Glossary/Index and its formula Appendix; calculator-keystroke asides (the mathematics is placed, the keystrokes are not); the curriculum statement's strand progression tables (printed pp. 81–120), which restate its coded outcomes; and Year 12's Unit 8 project unit, placed as one item. The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Turkmenistan's whole filed in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **5 pupil files = 3 pupil works, plus 1 teacher guide** (6 documents, 840 PDF pages in all) — and the expansion map's "3 student + 0 teacher" kind split is **wrong** (**flag F1**: the map counts one file per grade/track slot; it misses the second parts of both Turkmen works and the teacher guide entirely — any plan sized on "3 books" under-sizes the read by about 2.5×). The pupil works are the complete Turkmen-medium Grade 1 and Grade 2 courses — *Matematika* Parts 1 and 2 for each grade (Atabaýew & Öwezowa, 3rd editions, 2019/2020) — and Part 1 only of the Russian-medium Grade 1 course (Rulieva, Chernikova, Mavlyanova & Satdykova, 4th ed. 2022), a separate work by a separate author team, not a translation of the Turkmen pair; the two Grade 1 Turkmen books are scans with no text layer and were read from page renders. The one teacher book, N. Açilowa's *Başlangyç synplarda matematikany okatmagyň usulyýeti* (Methodology of Teaching Mathematics in the Primary Classes, Ylym, 2015), is a teacher-training study guide: it teaches NO pupil content, so its rows are **teacher evidence only** and count in no lesson's taught-in figure; the five pupil books are real pupil textbooks, recommended by the Ministry of Education of Turkmenistan and served by the Ministry's own E-kitaphana, so their rows **count**. **Flag F2** (record-side: reported here, not fixed; merges never edit the records, and no `maths/books/turkmenistan.md` filing exists): a `books.json` note claims the Russian Grade 1 Part 2 "is E-kitaphana post 507" — post 507 is in fact **Grade 2 Russian Mathematics Part 1** (per the enumeration table and the grid match), no Grade 1 Russian Part 2 post exists in the catalogue, and that Part 2 is recorded nowhere as found; post 507 must not be double-counted as both a Grade 1 and a Grade 2 book, and the Russian Grade 1 work stands in hand as Part 1 only. **Flag F3**: the filing notes for these books rested on samples ("every-fifth-page verification", contents lists not yet verified); this full re-read confirms the sampled claims spot-checked — e.g. the kilogram section of Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 2 does sit at printed pp. 63–64 — and supplies the missing contents lists for both Grade 2 parts (Part 1 contents at PDF p. 103; Part 2 at PDF pp. 118–119); no sampled content claim checked was found false. **Flag F4**: the map's "2 link-only" figure cannot be confirmed and looks far too low — the banked E-kitaphana grid match carries Maths links for the Grade 2 Russian pair and for Grades 3–9 Mathematics, Algebra and Geometry, plus a Grade 5 methodology guide; every one of those is link-only, not in hand and cited nowhere, and everything beyond Grade 2 is outside this set. Byte-identical duplicate posts are cited once, on the filed copy (post 403 = the Grade 1 Part 2 file; post 412 = the Grade 2 Part 1 file; post 418 = the Grade 2 Part 2 file; post 576 = the teacher guide's post 555 file), and every pupil file was pulled from its GitHub release asset at the exact recorded byte size (reconcile sweep #8 lists 23/23 Turkmenistan entries secured). Counting grain: one item per distinct teaching section of a book (its contents-list section, or a headed teaching block where the contents list runs broader), with consolidation/revision sections counted once per book-part; pure exercise pages inside a section are apparatus of that section's item. In every book in this set the printed folio equals the PDF page. One reading note, carried exactly: in the Grade 1 (Turkmen) Part 1 book's type, the empty answer box prints as a small square — in the PDF font layer it can appear as «*» (e.g. the comparing exercises "7 * 6" mean "put <, > or = between 7 and 6") — it is NOT a multiplication sign, and multiplication is not taught in that book. Not stocked as separate rows, exactly as the placements direct: the Grade 2 Part 1 money item 14 (manat notes and coins inside addition/subtraction stories at printed pp. 50, 61 — context only, no money section is taught in that book, and the placements count it inside items 11–13), and the four consolidation/revision items the placements assign to no lesson (Grade 2 Part 1 item 17, Grade 2 Part 2 item 42, Grade 1 Part 1 item 71, Grade 1 Russian Part 1 item 125) — practice over sections already stocked, no new content. Turkmenistan's class for Maths is **COURSES-CLOSED** on the as-filed set. Into this lesson the placements stock 5 items, all of them pupil.

## Tunisia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Tunisia's whole in-hand Maths set from the file — exactly 1 book, a teacher's guide, and no pupil textbook: the *Mathematics — Teacher's Book, Year 1 of Basic Education* (CNP code 502107P00). The expansion map is CORRECT for the in-hand set (0 student + 1 teacher), with one title refinement carried everywhere: the library/checklist title says “grades 1–2” because the checklist's one teacher-guide row spans both volumes, but this file is the **Year 1 volume only** — its cover names Year 1 alone, and the Year 2 volume is the separate file 502205P00. The guide is the only mathematics row for Tunisia in `textbooks.tsv` (75 Tunisia rows: Arabic, English, French, philosophy, Spanish and this one) and the only mathematics entry in any staging `books.json`; no Maths hides under another subject label and there is no “all subjects” Tunisia volume. The book was grabbed byte-exact through the authenticated GitHub API (9,205,715 bytes, 184 PDF pages, MD5 d017ae08ae7c35d335757b0fb1ef747f — matching the library's verified record for the complete Wayback capture of 502107P00; staging holds no Tunisia PDF on disk) and read in full from rendered pages — the PDF's text layer is glyph-encoded (extraction is mojibake) and Arabic OCR returned nothing on these watermarked scans; the diagonal CNP watermark does not hide body text. Printed folio = PDF page throughout. Counting grain: one item per distinct concept, skill or teaching point the guide itself teaches or explains, at the grain the guide presents it; lesson-plan conduct prose, activity scripts and assessment apparatus are not separate items unless they carry a distinct mathematical teaching point. Where the guide itself separates teacher background theory from what Year 1 pupils actually do, the item says so — the teacher-background items (16, 17, 28, 57, 58, 75, 76 and 77) are placed as teacher background, exactly as the placements word them. The placements' named exclusions are stocked nowhere: the life-skills chapter (printed pp. 55–67 — general life-skills/21st-century-skills pedagogy, no mathematics content); the error-remediation procedure, didactic-contract discussion and remediation planning (printed pp. 45–50 passim — teaching apparatus; the content-bearing error examples are placed as items); the model lesson plans (printed pp. 78–132) beyond the points placed from the distributed sequence; and the pupil-facing integration and problem-solving worksheets of Part Three (printed pp. 158–179), practice material enacting the placed concepts and introducing none the reference or sequence sections do not already teach. Record items R1–R5 are apparatus or exam evidence, stocked nowhere as teaching items: R1 the assessment typology — diagnostic before new learning, formative during, certifying at a period's or year's end (printed pp. 41–42); R2 the six period evaluations (printed pp. 134–152) — exam evidence only; R3 the closing Arabic–French glossary of mathematical terms (printed pp. 154–157); R4 the guide's statement (printed p. 158) that the pupil textbook is held to at most 120 pages, so the extra integration and problem-solving situations are printed in this guide instead — the pupil book and this guide are designed as one package; R5 the bibliography (printed pp. 180–182). Set boundary: the pupil textbooks for Years 1, 2 and 6 (102105P01, 102105P02, 102210P00, 102609P00), the Year 2 volume of this teacher's guide (502205P00) and all later-year najahni/exercice.tn material are exact-link only — recorded links, never downloaded, in no `books.json`, not in hand and not read. Tunisia is **TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide teaches or explains, Tunisia is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Tunisia's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (teacher-evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 8 teacher-evidence items from Book 1.

No Maths content hides under another subject label: the other 42 in-hand Zimbabwe files are the same PlusOne TG series in English, ChiShona, Science and Technology, Social Science and Physical Education and Arts — none is an "all subjects" volume and none carries Maths units. The ZIMSAKE learner's books once counted were proved publisher SAMPLERS and removed in 2026-11 — 8 of 8 ZIMSAKE files ever pulled are proven samplers, none is a Maths book and none is in hand; the Grade 7 Mathematics *Revision Guide* is not in hand and is an excluded class in any case (revision guides do not count). Counting grain: one item per contents sub-lesson (ECD guides, whose units are sequences of workbook-page lessons) or per numbered unit (Grades 1–7), split only where one unit plainly teaches two distinct course lessons. Pages cited are the books' printed pages (printed folio = PDF page − 4 in the ECD guides; Grades 1–7 unit starts are the contents printed pages, verified against the folios). Book errors found on the read are taught corrected and recorded: the Grade 4 contents lists Unit 17 Money at printed p. 59 and Unit 18 Time at p. 58 — the page order in the contents is reversed; the units themselves run Money (objectives printed p. 56) before Time, and the Money placement cites the objectives page; the Grade 6 Unit 23 (Time) guide cites its Learner's Book as "pages 18−204" — a dropped digit (the unit's own exercises sit at Learner's Book pp. 189–204), a citation slip in the guide that does not affect the teaching content; and the Grade 7 contents misnumbers the final Data handling unit as "Unit 21" although it follows Unit 30 — the body heads it Unit 31, and the placement records both numbers. The Grade 4 Unit 5 decimal comma (3,1 for three-and-a-tenth) is the book's consistent notation, recorded as printed, not treated as an error; decimals are written with the decimal comma throughout Grades 4–7. Record note N1: `phase3-push-state.json` still lists only 4 Zimbabwe folders (paperwork) although all 51 Zimbabwe files — including these 9 — are confirmed live on GitHub (sweep #8, 2026-11-05); the state-file gap is already on record, and this read re-confirms the 9 Maths files on GitHub at exact recorded sizes and hashes. Zimbabwe is **TEACHER-GUIDE EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the guide states the teacher teaches and what learners should be able to do, Zimbabwe is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Zimbabwe's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 teacher-evidence item(s).

## Zambia added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Zambia's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **13 files, 626 PDF pages, and every one of them teacher evidence: the in-hand set holds no pupil textbook at all**. The expansion map is WRONG for Zambia Maths on both numbers: it listed 2 student + 7 teacher, but the true recorded split is 2 student + 11 teacher (the map's teacher count omitted the 4 KG Pre-Mathematics teacher files), and on opening BOTH files recorded as "student" proved to be teaching modules — the Grade 7 "Mathematics 2" file is the *Mathematics II Teaching Module, Form 1 Term 2* (its preface states it "is designed to help teachers prepare and deliver Competence-Based lessons"), and the KG1 "Pre-Mathematics and Science" file is the *Intellectual Disabilities Teaching Module, Term 3* (its cover is titled "TEACHING MODULE" and its interior is teacher guidance with teacher/learner role tables and a lesson-plan appendix). The two kind mis-records live in `textbooks.tsv` and are flagged in the expansion diff for the record-keepers — record-side, reported here, not fixed by this merge (merges never edit the filings or records). The set: ECE Pre-Mathematics and Science teaching modules (KG1 mainstream Terms 2 and 3, KG2 mainstream Term 3, and the Intellectual Disabilities adaptations for Terms 2 and 3); combined Mathematics and Science teaching modules for Grade 1 (Terms 2 and 3) and Grade 2 (Term 3) — only their mathematics content is placed here, the science halves are the placements' named exclusions; the Grade 5 *Numeracy Teacher's Guide* of the Catch Up Programme (MoE/VVOB, 2023), whose TaRL method — assessing each learner one-to-one and teaching learners grouped by their current level, not their grade — is Zambia's own adopted way of teaching foundational numeracy; and pure Mathematics teaching modules for Grade 4 (Term 3) and Grade 7 (Mathematics I Terms 2 and 3, Mathematics II Term 2). All 13 files were fetched fresh for the read (12 via the authenticated GitHub Contents API from the textbooks repo tree; the Grade 5 Catch Up guide via its recorded VVOB source URL at exactly the recorded byte size), verified by byte size, MD5, pdfinfo page count and cover/title page, and read in full; all 13 MD5s differ — no duplicates. Not in hand and cited nowhere: the CDC Digital Library *Mathematics and Science Teaching Module* for Form 2 (visual-impairment), which exists only as a banked lead with no file, and every Zambian pupil textbook, which sits behind a commercial wall (Longman/ZEPH/Oxford/MK) with no free file — the one further free lead is itself another teaching module, so it would not change the class even if secured. Zambia's own teaching ways are placed as content in their own right: local materials, Zambian languages, kwacha money and TaRL level grouping. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected and recorded in the placements: the Grade 7 Mathematics II module swaps the definitions of Profit and Loss in its Key Terms (printed p. 66), prints "the sum of exterior and exterior angles add up to 180°" where interior + exterior is meant (printed p. 57), mixes K600 000 with K60 000 and asks for "the price of the bag" in a laptop question in its end-of-topic assessment (printed pp. 77–78), gives a bacterium "6 300 000 meters long" (printed p. 9), and its cover and imprint-page ISBNs disagree; the Grade 7 Mathematics I Term 3 module's Pythagoras assignment sends a walker 3 km east and then 4 km **west** (printed p. 3) — as written no 3–4–5 triangle arises; the Grade 7 Mathematics I Term 2 module gives Lusaka–Kitwe as 845 km in one bullet and Kitwe–Lusaka as 357 km in another (printed pp. 15–16), prints "Complementary angles add up to 900" for 90° (printed p. 27), and carries a simplifying tip that reads as though unlike terms can be added or subtracted — only like terms combine (printed p. 6); the Grade 5 Catch Up guide leaves its own unfinished editorial query "(please check this line for meaning)" standing in the printed division lesson (printed p. 41) — the line it queries is still understandable and is placed as written; and the Grade 2 module's subtraction demonstration names the wrong character ("take 13 sticks from Samuel", PDF p. 37 — the module's characters are Bupe and Mutinta) and first asks for 13 sticks where its own word problem needs 15 (PDF p. 23). Zambia is **TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY** in every lesson: every item is worded as what the module or guide states the teacher teaches, Zambia is named in the countries line and in the rows below, and it is added to NO lesson's acceptance count, on the Ghana precedent. Into this lesson the placements stock 2 teacher-evidence item(s).

**Book 1:** *Pre-Mathematics and Science Teaching Module, Level 1 (ages 3–4), Term 2* — Zambia, Curriculum Development Centre, Lusaka, 2025; 1,624,150 bytes, 78 PDF pp., MD5 96dbd98f3f4bf99453202d2358d08ce8. Teaching module — TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY. The module's pages carry no printed folios, so PDF pages are cited.

**Book 2:** *Pre-Mathematics and Science Teaching Module for Learners with Intellectual Disabilities, Level 1 (ages 3–4), Term 2* — Zambia, Curriculum Development Centre with the Zambia Institute of Special Education, Lusaka, 2025; 1,739,926 bytes, 32 PDF pp., MD5 0db94e3ed6153749fbe36ac28537031d. Teaching module — TEACHER EVIDENCE ONLY: the special-education adaptation of the Level 1 module, with smaller number ranges and more repetition than the mainstream module. The module's pages carry no printed folios, so PDF pages are cited.

Zambia's class for Maths is **PARTIAL (evidence-only)**: all 13 in-hand Maths files have been read in full and placed, and it advances only when a genuine Zambian pupil Maths book lands in hand and is read.

## Uruguay added 2026-11-11 (expansion merge)

The 2026-11-10 expansion read read Uruguay's whole in-hand Maths set from the PDFs — **6 books: five pupil WORKBOOKS of the official series *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática* (ANEP · DGEIP · CACEEM) — Inicial, Primero (5th ed. 2021), Segundo, Tercero and Quinto (5th ed. 2021) — plus one teacher book, *Matemática en el Segundo Ciclo — Libro para el Maestro* (Grades 4–6)** — and placed 340 items (304 pupil + 36 teacher-evidence). The expansion map's count is right and its kind label is wrong (flag F1 — record-side, reported here, not fixed by this merge; merges never edit the filings or the records): the five pupil books are activity **workbooks**, not student textbooks. A workbook is a pupil book (the South Africa/Peru pattern): the pupil-workbook items below are **teaching content**, so Uruguay's pupil rows are COUNTED in full. The teacher book's items are **teacher evidence only** — worded as teacher evidence, named in the countries line and in the rows, and added to no acceptance count (the Ghana precedent); the teacher book is also the only in-hand coverage of the Grade 4 and Grade 6 pupil content, whose workbooks are not in hand. **Not in hand, not read, cited nowhere**: the Grade 4 (Cuarto) pupil workbook (36,381,739 B) and the Grade 6 (Sexto) pupil workbook (50,368,322 B) — official DGEIP URLs banked, but every banked Wayback capture is crawler-truncated at 5,242,880 B, the live DGEIP host does not answer from this network, and the Grade 4 book also circulates only as a login-gated Studocu copy (not pursued, per the no-login rule) — and the Primer Ciclo teacher book (Inicial–Grade 3, 5,703,330 B), likewise link-only (captures truncated at 1,048,576 B). Record flag F2 (record-side, reported, not fixed): `textbooks.tsv` carries no row for the Grade 3 workbook, although `phase3-push-state.json` records it as pushed and its staging folder carries a books.json — the book is in hand and was read in full. The Grade 5 workbook is an image-only PDF and was read from page renders; the other five books were read from their text layers. Book errors found on the read are taught corrected: the Grade 3 workbook's garden table (printed p. 79) prints last year's figures 40, 35 and 20 with a total of 85 — the rows sum to 95; the Grade 5 workbook's worked example (printed p. 84) decomposes 0.65 as 6/10 + 9/100, which makes 0.69 — corrected to 6/10 + 5/100 (the decimal form printed beside it, 0.65 = 0.6 + 0.05, is correct); and the Grade 5 book's printed p. 14 side tab names the wrong guide character, "Lechuza" for this book's "Yacaré" — a production error only, content unaffected. (The Grade 5 book's p. 116 "almost two and a half times more casein" line is NOT an error: it is a pupil claim the book itself asks children to judge, and it is placed as such.) The placements' named exclusions are apparatus, stocked nowhere: the workbooks' cut-out pages (recortables), the Inicial personal pages (printed pp. 8–9) and its ice-melting observation page (printed p. 78 — science, no maths content), and the Grade 5 opening questionnaire, guide-character pages and recortables. Printed-page basis: printed page = PDF page − 2 in all six books. Counting grain: one item per distinct activity / teaching point in a workbook; one item per distinct teaching guidance / content statement in the teacher book. Into this lesson the placements stock 4 items — 4 pupil-workbook rows.

**Book 1:** *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Inicial* (pupil workbook) — Uruguay, ANEP · CEIP/DGEIP · CACEEM, 1st edition, ISBN 978-9974-677-76-0; 7,366,728 bytes, 99 PDF pp., MD5 b7f3dbb92271a6dd4cb89140d6d128f7. PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted: the workbook teaches each topic through activities and guided practice. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Guide character: Lobito.

**Book 4:** *Cuaderno para Hacer Matemática en Tercero* (pupil workbook) — Uruguay, ANEP · CEIP/DGEIP · CACEEM; 5,093,386 bytes, 100 PDF pp., MD5 84d0f4c53413f2739d9b418aeaf69066. PUPIL WORKBOOK — counted. Printed page = PDF page − 2. Guide character: Guazubirá. (Record flag F2: `textbooks.tsv` carries no row for this book although push-state records it as pushed — record-side, reported, not fixed.)
